# Android parity benchmark 2026-10-25

Device: sdk_gphone64_x86_64 (API 35), WebView com.google.android.webview, 154.0.8037.106. Toggle samples: 30. Emission runs: 200. Independent trials: 5.

APK policy: distinct application IDs dev.ngrxsqlite.harness.classic and dev.ngrxsqlite.harness.sqlite; each APK installed once before trials. --skip-build reuses .tmp/benchmark-apks/<impl>.apk.
